- **2026-10-14, F20 BUILT (NOT FLOWN; flies with F19).** The game's own model mode, with the native first-person view kept.
  Approved by Sean. Temporary key `fix.native_on_foot_body_mode` (game | third; default game; F8 row on the native on-foot page).
  RE, build 332841 (checked in the Epic exe with capstone; the scratch script is not in the repo):
  - `HumanoidModelManager::SetMode` is at EliteDangerous64.exe+0x1A112B0, vtable slot 0x30 of the manager whose vtable is +0x5200F90. Its first
    22 bytes `40 53 48 83 EC 20 48 8B D9 39 91 30 02 00 00 75 05 45 84 C0 74 1F` (push rbx; sub rsp,0x20; mov rbx,rcx; cmp [rcx+0x230],edx; ...).
    Args: rcx = manager, edx = mode (0 first-person models, 1 third-person), r8b = force. The mode is stored at [manager+0x230].
  - The per-frame hook is the povCamera joint handler at EliteDangerous64.exe+0x19B1240, entered for every humanoid. Its first 32 bytes
    `40 53 48 83 EC 20 48 85 D2 48 89 74 24 30 48 8D B2 B0 01 00 00 B8 A0 02 00 00 48 0F 44 F0 48 8D` match the brief. The manager is
    `[rdx+0x1B0]` (rdx = the receiver). Local player only: `[manager+0x238] != 0` and `[manager] == base+0x5200F90`.
  - Conflict check: Explorer Cam's FindJoint sites are return addresses +0x19B12D5 and +0x19B1359, both past the 6 bytes this hook steals
    (+0x19B1240..+0x19B1245, `push rbx; sub rsp,0x20`). No other hook in the tree touches either entry.
  Mechanism: the SetMode detour (a callback relay, ecm::buildCallbackRelay) records the game's requests for the captured local manager and,
  while the body is wanted as third person, forwards a request for 0 as 1 (as 0 while aiming down sights; ADS is Status.json Flags2 bit 5,
  `journalAds()` beside the F16 breathable flag). A request for 1 is never changed. The povCamera hook runs the original first, then holds
  the local manager at the desired mode through the SetMode trampoline, so EDVR's own calls never reach the detour. When the hold ends
  (key to game, native ended, session ended, shutdown) the game's own mode is restored once. The game's mode is the last game request,
  or the value read before the first hold when no request was seen. A captured manager not seen for 60 frames is dropped without a restore.
  The two hooks install when the key first reads third, after the identity check, the vtable slot check and both prologue checks; the
  relays are placed within two gigabytes; nothing is patched if any check fails. Flat profile: refused.
  Logs (the hook threads never log; they queue, and the frame boundary writes):
  - install: `native on foot body mode: installed -- SetMode at EliteDangerous64.exe+0x1A112B0 ..., stolen=N bytes, prologue 22 bytes verified, relay at 0x.. (thread T)` and the povCamera line with 32 bytes.
  - `native on foot body mode: game asked M (thread T) -> forwarded M'` (on a change only)
  - `native on foot body mode: set M (ADS a, frame thread T2)` (each EDVR call)
  - `native on foot body mode: restored to M (reason, frame thread T2)`
  Read it this way: a `game asked 0 -> forwarded 1` with `set 1` and no `restored` is the hold working; an install line with no `set` line
  means the local manager was never captured (the `local third-person humanoid seen` line says it was).
  Note: the first run of this build will not show the body's model change in the logs, only the calls; the visible result is the flight's.
  F19 keys are unchanged. F19 and F20 fly together: the body clip (F19) and the model mode (F20) are separate hooks.
  Build: source commit 816c1d80. The first full build failed on two duplicate `flags2` declarations in journal_watch.cpp, left by merging main into HEAD (65cdfc7b); the commit removes the duplicates (identical copies). The second full build passed (`all gates passed`, receipt written). The shared build lock was held by other worktrees for about an hour before it started.

### 2026-10-14: F19/F20 FLOWN, F21 BUILT

- **F19/F20 flight (log edvr_gfx_20261014_172807.log).** Third mode (F20) gave the full third-person body and the weapon in the hands, correct. The HUD was lost and there was no ADS pose: the Flags2 ADS bit (0x20) was never set in third. The game itself set the torso and arm vertex-clip enable to 0 on the third-person Torso, Arms, Hands and Shoulders in third mode and back to 1 in first-person mode. That confirms F19's unclip mechanism. The pilot prefers first-person mode with a hybrid body (F21).
- **Ammo display and waypoint.** Both follow the head. The first-person rig is anchored to the head camera, and the HUD canvas icons are world-projected. Research running.
- **Indicators on a planet.** Key 10 (about 5 m, the aim reticle and weapon display) and key 43 (waypoint, about 380 m). The reticle writer touches only key 10.
- **STEP 0 (merge check).** The full build on the merged HEAD 2cf59dbb passed unchanged; no merge fix was needed. The `FAIL:` lines printed by the screen-motion mutation run sit inside the gate's own output and the gate passed.
- **F21 (commit a68ba714, NOT FLOWN).**
  - Reticle `game`: EDVR writes nothing to key 10. The stub stays installed so a head placement made before a switch is handed back once.
  - Hybrid body `fix.native_on_foot_body_hybrid` (TEMPORARY, default off): the first-person skeleton's masks on the mode-2 AMC; the third-person torso unclip and the arms, hands and shoulders' masks on the mode-3 AMC. It overrides the two body keys while on.
  - Eye probe P1: raw-inputs line added (`native on foot eye inputs:`).
- **P1 eye-vs-body: bug not found.** Read against Explorer Cam's convention (explorer_cam_core.h: +0x3B0 rows right, up, forward; origin in row 3; dot with rows), the probe's arithmetic is right: the AMC matrix is row-vector with its origin at +0x30, and the head translation is read from row 3 of the +0x58 matrix. The open candidates, each tested by the new line:
  - (a) Frame of the job's camera floats. Explorer Cam's commander frame has its own `root` (commanderFrame: root = W.origin - L.origin x F), not the AMC origin. If the camera floats are commander-local or rebased, `|cam - origin|` will not be about eye height.
  - (b) Frame of the head joint. The +0x58 matrix is avatar-root space, and the AMC frame equals that root only if the root sits at the AMC origin. The line's `|head_model|` (the standing head is about 1.66 m in root space) must match `|head world - origin|` if the basis is orthonormal.
  - (c) The head sample is up to 100 ms old, which is too small to explain 0.7 m.
  - Scale: the row lengths in the line show whether the basis is unit length.
- **Next.** Flight F21 on Epic with `reticle_follow = game` and `body_hybrid = on`. Check the census lines and the `eye inputs` line on foot.
### 2026-10-14: F22 built (commit a2a544d4)

- **Part A, sessions.** `journalOnFoot()` reads Status.json Flags2 bit 0 (cleared when the file has no Flags2), so the fallback is the same signal the log already showed. `journalSessionOrOnFoot` admits on LoadGame or on foot now. Native on foot (setter and frame) and the Explorer Cam hotkey use it. Other `journalGameplay()` users are unchanged.
- **Part C, look to aim.** Bytes checked against the Epic exe with capstone before writing: update +0x19973D0 (32 bytes, steal 7), processor +0x1A9C370 (5 bytes, steal 5, single exit; store of the yaw delta at +0x5F4 and pitch at +0x5F8; basis rows 4-float stride at +0x5B0). The processor's return is unused at its call site (+0x1A19626), so a void wrapper is safe. The relay only uses RAX, so xmm1 (dt) reaches the callback.
- **Runtime.** Frame-output version 6 adds `lookYawTotal` and `lookYawGameFrame`. The client asks 6 first and steps down; test rigs (native_frame_test, comfort_fade_cases, explorer_cam_test) follow the new sizes. The compensation is a rotation about the head applied to a separate seated pose (`lookSeated`); `currentOrigin` stays the seat memory's origin, and `applySeatedResetCore` re-bases the total. A `look_to_aim_runtime` trace line carries the runtime frame and the game frame.
- **Build.** First two gate failures were mine and are fixed: the settings schema needs `live` on every temporary key's `ui:` line, and a trace format string had a raw newline. The full build then passed ("Native OpenXR build and all gates passed", receipt 88c79c36...).
- **Not verified in a headset.** Whether the runtime's yaw sign matches the game's (flight check (a) above), the processor's delta semantics beyond the store, and the gain's linearity. Each is a line in the flight log, not a claim.
- **Ruled out (static, Part D):** the game camera includes the HMD pose and the first-person rig reads it. Open hypotheses H1-H3 are in the Status block; probes A and B are the test.

### 2026-10-14: F22 flight and F23 built

- **F22 flight (19:15).** Part A confirmed in Training. Part C spun the commander: the first engaged error read -170.73 deg, and centred-head samples read ±160..180 deg, so forward was the backward axis (atan2(C[8], C[10]) is backward). Fixed in F23 as forward = -row 2. Ruled out: head-relative yaw = atan2(row2.x, row2.z) of C, because a centred head read ±170..180 deg (F22). The basis element swung -42 -> +38 with the view pitch near 0, so the pitch probe read a non-pitch element. The hybrid body (F21) gave Sean a torso at 19:20:20; the arms are the open question.
- **F23 (built, commit 1abc4899).** Yaw convention, rate cap 180 deg/s × dt, the runaway guard (270 deg in 3 s; at cap 1 s with the error not falling; stays off until the key toggles or on foot re-enters), the compensation sign instrument and `fix.native_on_foot_look_to_aim_comp_sign` (TEMPORARY), the full-basis pitch probe.
- **Part E (built, same commit).** Read-only stage timers on the load dispatcher, EliteDangerous64.exe+0x1CA6510 (16 bytes verified; steal 8; relay preserves rdx and xmm2; RAX only). Table at +0x5E7B460, 21 entries, stride 0x30; per-stage record at obj+0x2B0+0x18*i (int state 0 waiting, 1 running, 2 done; float start; float end); clock at obj+0x4C4; net gate dword at [obj+0x4B8]+0xC0 (>= 5 to start a stage). The stage names, in table order:
  0 WatchForQuit, 1 EstablishServers, 2 WaitForEDServer, 3 EnsureEDServerConnected, 4 CheckForDisconnectRequest, 5 PreviousLocationShutdown, 6 DestinationLocationFailure, 7 RootDataValidation, 8 VSOCalculation, 9 UpdateSkyBox, 10 ProvingGroundSetup, 11 ProvingGroundCheckForAbortLoad, 12 CreateLocationObjects, 13 CreatePlayerVessels, 14 WaitForPendingAISpawns, 15 EnterMultiCrew, 16 CreatePointOfFocus, 17 PlanetarySurfaceGeneration, 18 PersistentAsteroidGeneration, 19 WaitForRemoteMachines, 20 EnterLocationFinalStep.
- **Not verified in a headset.** The runtime sign (comp_sign), the controller's error after the convention change, and whether the guard holds under a real turn. Each is a log line in the F23 flight check.

### 2026-10-14: F23 flight, F24 unbuilt

- **F23 flight (19:40).** The guard tripped 1.5 s after engaging. Raw C row 2 = (-0.0613, -0.1530, 0.9863) with the head centred: forward = -row 2 read 176 deg, +row 2 read -3.6 deg. So F22's convention was right, and the F23 diagnosis was wrong. Ruled out: forward = -row 2 of C. F22's spin is most likely the yaw sign (the error grew to the wrap, which is the signature of a turn opposite to the input).

### 2026-10-15: F40 (view map, calibration, walk neck, live now pose)

- **Built, not installed.** Full build green. The F40 rig cases pass (the map inverts; a 34 cm body error closes monotonically to under 1 cm in 180 frames at psi 0, 37 and 180 deg; psi; the head's body-axes map; the flag on version 9 output).
- **Neck fallback (no walk on foot).** When no candidate name answers `FindJoint`, the census reads the head joint's parent from the skeleton's pose arrays, once per skeleton (`neckWalkIndex`, its own buffers; Explorer Cam's walk is not called). The walk enumerates joint indices and parents, not names, so no joint-name list can be logged from it. The `neck joint ...` line names the walk parent when it is used.
- **Per-part table.** Once per body entry, the shift pass prints only the shifted records (block, slot, part name, mask), then a count line: parts present, parts shifted, records shifted and skipped.
- **View map (replaces the C-chain of F37).** seated = D . Ry(psi) . err_body, with D = diag(-1,-1,+1). err_body is the target minus the camera (the trims are in the target); the sign follows the measured plant (cam_body moves by D . applied at psi 0), so the loop closes on the rig's plant. The head's motion in body axes is Ry(psi)^-1 . diag(1,1,-1) . tS. psi = seated head yaw (render row 2) less the headset's yaw in the body (C row 2 less the AMC basis row 2). The psi sign and the z column are not measured: the calibrate flight decides them. The `view map:` line prints psi every 5 s.
- **Calibrate (`fix.native_on_foot_body_align = calibrate`, one flight).** Six 1 s steps: +5 cm seated X, rest, +5 cm Y, rest, +5 cm Z, rest, repeating. No loop, no guard: the offsets are scheduled. At the start of each step the step that just ended is measured: the camera's body position and tS, the mean over its last 0.5 s less the mean over the 0.5 s before it began (`calibrate step ...`). The body<-seated 3x3 comes from the three active steps.
- **Live now pose (`fix.now_pose = next | render`, live).** The d3d11 key goes to the runtime through the native frame output, version 9 (version 8 plus one word, `nowPoseRender`). The client asks 9 first and steps down to 8 for an older d3d11.dll. The runtime's `g_nowPoseRender` (head_pose_time.h) picks the answer: next (default) locates Elite's "now" request one display period past the latest frame's display time, as today; render locates it at that display time itself (the WaitGetPoses latch's instant). With render, the pose cache's display answers are not used (its slots are one period ahead), so every display call takes the owner path. Each change is logged on both sides (`now pose: ...`), and the runtime logs its default at start (`native_now_pose_config`).
- **pose_cache and frame_end_overlap** stay per-launch keys in edvr_openxr.ini (already read at startup, already logged at start). They are not made live; a relaunch flips them.
- **Marker angles.** Every 5 s in the marker path: M (read at entry, F37) against this call's list-B view, and against the runtime's now answer (`M against C ... M against the now answer ...`). That shows which pose M was built from.
- **Overseer note.** The `next` answer may not be what fixed terrain black squares (the TERRAIN CULL COVER may have). now_pose is an open choice; Sean watches for black terrain squares with render.
- **Deviations.** The `neck-to-camera` offset is logged, not applied (F39's interpretation stands). The view map's psi sign and the z column are unverified until the calibrate flight.

### 2026-10-15: F40 flight record (log 105206, dump eye_105603)

- **Calibration proved the map.** +X 5 cm gives cam_body (-0.050, 0, 0); +Y gives (0, -0.050, 0); +Z gives (0, 0, +0.050); off-diagonals under 4 mm. The head's motion maps as diag(1,1,-1) . tS. psi is zero; F40's viewPsi moved with head turns and was wrong.
- **The log's own calibration steps are noisy.** The rest steps drift 2 to 9 cm in cam_body, and psi moves with head turns (-24 to -49 deg). The dump analysis is the basis for the map, not the step deltas alone.
- **Body bobbing is the controller.** Body is open loop (the neck is unshifted, the records move). The F37 integral (0.2 a frame, about 14 a second) into a critically damped 250 ms filter has about -210 degrees of phase at crossover: a limit cycle (d swings +-20 cm up, +-12 cm right, period 1.6 to 8 s) with cam and neck constant.
- **Per-part table.** It did print: once, on the first shifted pass after the body entry (10:55:30.584): EVASuit_Torso (block 27), EVASuit_Legs (30), EVASuit_Feet (31), EVASuit_Gear_Back (48), with the count line. The F41 build prints the same table with the request line on entry.
- **Neck by name, confirmed.** `def_c_neck_joint` answered; pov minus neck (-0.005, 0.118, 0.049) m. The walk-parent fallback stays.

### 2026-10-15: F41 (view map, body filter, per-part table)

- **View map.** psi is zero (`nofoot::viewPsi` is removed): seated = D . err_body, D = diag(-1,-1,+1); the head's motion is diag(1,1,-1) . tS. Look_to_aim's seated yaw is not supplied yet (TODO in the code and the log).
- **View loop, instability check.** The view loop is closed (the camera follows the applied offset 250 ms late), so it cannot be made open loop. At the F37 gain 0.2 and a 250 ms filter it is unstable for the same reason as body (about 14 a second integral into a lag). Replaced: integral 0.02 a frame into one critically damped filter, tau 0.1 s, 5 cm a frame at most. The rig's delayed simulation (22 frames) closes a 34 cm error under 1 cm in 5 s.
- **Body filter (no integral).** target = (cam - pov) - trims in body axes, the camera low-passed at 0.1 s and the pov at 0.4 s, then one critically damped filter (tau 0.1 s, 5 cm a frame at most, 0.6 m clamp). The rig: a 20 cm step in camera height is monotone, overshoots under 5%, and settles within 1 s. `body axes` line once a second: target and d (right, up, forward).
- **Rig.** The three measured steps within 5 mm, seatedFromBody(err, 0) = (-ex, -ey, +ez), the head's diag(1,1,-1), the body step, and the delayed view loop.
- **Per-part table.** Requested on entering body (a line says so); printed on the next shifted pass, one line per shifted record plus the count line.
- **Not verified.** Whether standing up raises the body needs a flight: the rig checks the response to a camera step, not the head's own motion. The look-to-aim psi (TODO) is unverified.

### 2026-10-15: F42 (frame-locked head pose, now_pose = render_locked)

- **Why.** Flight evidence: now_pose render plus forward is the most stable marker; pose_cache off is much worse. The design pass found at least four head samples per frame: the latched render pose, the game's pose one period on, every "now" query re-located at display time (under render those bypass the cache's display slots), and the cache slots.
- **The third state.** `fix.now_pose = next | render | render_locked` (live). The frame output's version 9 word carries 0, 1 or 2; the runtime's `g_nowPoseMode` (head_pose_time.h) holds it. render_locked answers every display "now" call with the latched render pose of the frame.
- **The latch.** The render pose is kept in PoseFrame at the publish point of waitPoses. The pose cache's record gets a `frame` slot (target = the display time). publishPoseCache runs for render_locked even with pose_cache off, takes no locate for its now and next slots, and reuses the record's keys and invalidation, so a withdrawn publication is never served.
- **The answer.** locateHeadFor tries the frame slot first (display call, seated origin); a miss falls through to the cache and the hop and is counted as a fallback.
- **The game pose.** Under render_locked waitPoses sets `game = render` (time included), so the game array and GetLastPoses(ForTrackedDeviceIndex) read the same sample. The separate locate of the game's pose is skipped.
- **Proof line.** `head pose samples per frame: mean ..., max ... distinct; now answers: latched ..., other ...; fallbacks from the latched path ...; angle_delta p95 ... deg` once a second (to the trace, not the gap sink). The samples: the render latch, the game array and each display "now" answer, hashed per frame (the bytes of the pose). Expect 1 distinct, 0 fallbacks, 0.000 degrees at p95 under render_locked. GetLast* read the same snapshot as the render and game samples, so they are not hashed separately.
- **Deviations.** The proof line is on its own line at the gap's cadence (not appended to the gap line), so the rig's gap-line count is unchanged. The rig does not exercise render_locked (the default is next); the flight is the test.
- **Risk.** The terrain cull (display-time answers caused black squares on fast turns before the cull cover). Sean will fly a fast planet-surface turn with render_locked and watch for black squares.

The log prints each name's index (0xFFFF: none). When a side's shoulder does not resolve, the pose walk is dumped: the 30 joints nearest the neck by index, with name hash, depth and parent.

The 3P shoulders through the AMC matrix. The target in body axes is (FP shoulder mean, low-passed 0.1 s) minus (3P shoulder mean, low-passed 0.4 s) minus the trims, through F41's single critically damped filter. Neck while a skeleton's shoulders do not resolve.

The per-part shifted list prints on each body entry (F42's log has it: four shifted parts and the count line).

### 2026-10-15: F44 (draw-time marker instrument, measure only)

- **Key.** `fix.native_on_foot_marker_probe` (off, hidden, live, TEMPORARY; VR only). Nothing drawn changes; no constant buffer, pool or binding is written. The machinery is `src\d3d11\draw_record_tag.h`, meant for the weapon ammo readout next.
- **Tag.** reticleWorldPost writes each world marker's t' (not key 10) with a 24-bit sentinel in the lowest 8 mantissa bits of x, y and z: the key (8 bits) and the render frame (16 bits), salted. Under 2^-15 relative per component; a zero, denormal or non-finite component is left untagged (counted). The tag, t' as written and P = node+0x50 go to the render thread through per-key sequence-counted atomic cells.
- **Find.** A t33 pool is watched once an on-foot HUD draw (VS 95D01BA609BF7500 or 46546443FD3C3F88) binds it; it must be a structured buffer of stride 336. At each Unmap of a DISCARD or NO_OVERWRITE map, +16 of each record is checked for the sentinels of the last 8 frames: the last-found index per key first, then the whole mapped range with 16-byte streaming loads (at most 4 scans a frame; over 2 ms backs off 30 frames). Each find keeps (pool, record index, X0).
- **Match.** Each HUD draw whose pool has a find (this frame or the last) copies its b0 rows 9-11, b1 row 275 and instance stream entries (the single stride-8 buffer at StartInstanceLocation; v0.x) into a staging slot; the first pool-family draw of each eye a frame (eye by the depth probe) copies the world b0 rows 9-11 and b1 row 275. Read back 2 or more frames later without a wait. When FirstElement + v0.x is a find: v0 = V_hud (X0 - o_hud), v_target = V_w (P - o_w).
- **Log, once a second.** `marker record found at index N` (key, tag age, map type, finds at the last index, index same or changed, X0 == t' bit-exact or not, scan times), or `sentinel NOT found`; per eye the angle v0 vs v_target (mean, max), the V_hud vs V_w rotation, head speed from the ring (mean, max), and the frame check |P - o_w| beside |t'| and |X0 - o_hud|; a draws line with each refusal counted (stride, stream, constants, no find, no world view, rows not a rotation, and so on). Each refusal reason is also named once.
- **Deviations.** V_hud and V_w are GPU copies of what each draw binds (ranged binds included), not a binding shadow plus mapped contents: the copy is what the draw reads, and no CPU copy of every b0 write is made. A HUD draw with no scene depth bound takes the eye whose world rows are nearest (counted as "eye by nearest world rows").
- **Not done.** No correction. The flight is the test: whether the sentinel survives into the pool at all, the record index's stability, and the angle at rest and while turning.

### 2026-10-15: F47 (the gated per-eye marker correction)

- **Why.** The F46 flight: the capture works for eye 0 only (eye 1: "the record was read for the other eye only" every second). |ndc_marker - ndc_P| is a constant 36.3 px (the icon's design height above P, not corrected) plus about 0.2 px per deg/s of head speed (about one frame of lag).
- **What changed.** New key `fix.native_on_foot_marker_fix` (off | on, hidden, live, TEMPORARY; on turns the probe on with it). The draw tee decides at each B10B032BDFD46700 draw: both eyes' marker draws matched in the read-backs of the last 8 frames, this draw the eye's matching one (its place among the eye's B10B draws), b0 a whole bind from constant 0 with a CPU shadow (Map/Unmap and UpdateSubresource tees), o_hud from engine motion's CB shadow, the eye's world rows from the CB shadow this frame, P from the find of the matched key. (dx, dy) = ndc(P, world b1 270..273 at o_w) - ndc(P, b0 rows 4, 5, 7 at o_hud) (`drawtag::ndcDelta`, rig-checked); gate |dx|, |dy| < 0.1 NDC and both w > 0; the last good delta is held one frame on a miss. Inside the UI layer's bracket (vscreen's shared draw path, after `uiLayerBegin`), the game's whole b0 goes into a work buffer, rows 4 and 5 (+ d . row 7) are written over it, the work buffer is copied whole into a private DEFAULT b0, which is bound for the game's issue and the layer's second issues; the game's b0 goes back after them. Both buffers are made once. Every refusal draws unpatched.
- **Item 2 (eye 1).** Every B10B draw is recorded whatever its pool; a stride-336 pool it binds is learned and watched; one line a frame (the first 8 read-back frames each second) lists each B10B draw's eye and source, place, call, instances, t33, watched or not, and the record and key it read (or why none). A second post of a key in one frame is tagged with bit 7 in the key (32 tag cells now); every record carrying the newest tag is a find (a second copy is counted); last frames' find indices are hints, and a census scan runs every 30 frames.
- **What it logs, once a second.** `marker fix (F47, key on|off): eye N:` the unpatched delta of P (dx, dy mean and max in px of the target, degrees mean and max), head speed, patched draws (held), the delta through the patched rows read back (about zero when the patch took), each not-applied reason (eye 0 / eye 1 unmatched, another draw, ranged b0, no CPU b0, o_hud or world view, no P, w <= 0, gate, bind refused), whether the patched draw read the record at read-back, and the CPU b0 shadow against the GPU copy. Said once: "eye N unmatched: not applied". A census line: B10B draws, pools learned, second posts, second copies, census scans, shadow writes, stale and unissued decisions.
- **Deviations.** "Matched" is the read-back's verdict 2 to 8 frames old, not the same frame (a stream match needs the GPU copy). The bracket is in vscreen's draw path around the issue, not inside `uiLayerBegin`, so the layer's second issues get the same b0. The rows go in through a work buffer, not a boxed UpdateSubresource on the constant buffer (that needs D3D11.1 partial updates).
- **Not done.** Not flown. With F46's eye 1 the gate holds the patch off; the item 2 lines say how eye 1 gets its marker.

### 2026-10-15: F49 (the pose freeze: Animate post-hook, probe by default)

- **What changed.** A post-hook on the frAnim Animate handler at 0x741520 (vtable 0x510E370 slot +8; rdx = the job, job[0] = the evaluator X). The 15 prologue bytes are checked against the loaded image; 7 are stolen (sub rsp,0x38; mov r10,rdx; no RIP-relative operand); a mismatch installs nothing, with a line. The hook acts only when job[0] is the local avatar's X: the census (job thread, every 100 ms) reads X from the local 3P skeleton interface (RR *(iface+0x2B8), AO *(iface+0x120)) and, once per X, builds the plan: P = [X+8], the frozen set F (the torso-side child subtree of the lowest common ancestor a of the neck 34 and def_l_foot_joint; the root's child on the neck's path when the foot does not resolve) and each member's rest record (P+0x48). Output span: [job+0x28] when [X+0x210] is 0, else [[X+0x1F0]+0xA0] when count-1 < [[X+0x1F0]+0xE0]; 0x43FB740 is never called; count = the u16 at P, cap 0xC0. On the worker: no allocation, logging or locks (a try-flag skips a second local call), every read and write under SEH; statistics go through a sequence counter to the frame thread.
- **Key.** `fix.native_on_foot_pose_freeze` = off | probe (default) | on; hidden, live, TEMPORARY. probe writes no game memory. on writes M'_j = local_j x M'_parent(j) for F in parent-first order, anchored at M_anim(a), the local blended from the animated toward the rest record (quaternion nlerp, position lerp) by a weight that moves over 0.3 s; the weight rises only while not ADS (journalAds) and the animated head (joint 44, read in the hook before any write) has held its height within 2 cm for 1 s. A faulted write stands the writes down for the session. Pure math in `native_on_foot_core.h` (`pfzFrozenSet`, `pfzRestModel`, `pfzBlendRecord`, `PfzBlend`, `PfzHeadRest`), checked in native_frame_test.
- **What it logs.** Once per X: `pose freeze: plan for X=...` (P against GetPoseData, the joint count, the foot's index, a, the torso child, F's member count and list, whether 35, 36, 34 and 44 are in F); the rest chain against the game's own 0xFDE0D0 for 34 and 44 (a leaf that reads P and writes 64 bytes; called only when its entry bytes match). Every 5 s on foot: Animate calls, local calls, the thread id and thread changes, [X+0x210], [X+0x12C], [X+0x128], [X+0x212], calls per output buffer, the count, joints 34 and 44 animated minus the rest chain anchored at a (mean, min, max and mean vector, mm), the weight and frames written. Every 5 s from the census: GetModelMatrix(34) against the hook's newest span matrix of 34 (and the written one when on), with the age. Each hook refusal is named once.
- **Risks.** A weapon attached to a 3P hand would float away from a frozen hand. The skin is uploaded from the t2 rows (APPLY_BIND_POSE) and the function that packs those rows was not found, so the skin may read another buffer: then on changes the animated matrices and nothing visible changes (not confirmed either way; the span cross-check says only whether GetModelMatrix sees the write). The write lands after [X+0x12C] = 1, so a reader that rotates the ring in the meantime may miss it. The bind pose of the arms may be a T or A pose.
- **Not done.** Not flown, not installed. The frame thread drains the statistics every 5 s, not at 1 Hz. Only the 3P skeleton's X is used (the local AMC's own interface is not read separately).

### 2026-10-15: F50 (the marker lag delta against a per-eye baseline; the body's vertical lock)

- **Why (F47 flight, log edvr_gfx_20261015_141552).** Both eyes matched (eye 0 to record 8, eye 1 to record 9), but the fix was gated off every frame: F47 projected the world point P through the marker's b0, which lives in the tracking (seat) frame, about 54 degrees from the world's. ruled out: P through the marker's b0 as the delta, because b0 is in the seat frame, not the world's.
- **What changed (marker, `fix.native_on_foot_marker_fix`).** `drawtag::ndcLagDelta`: ndc_marker = the record's X0 through the draw's own b0 rows 4, 5, 7 at o_hud; ndc_P = P through the eye's world b1 rows 270..273 at o_w; m = ndc_marker - ndc_P; (dx, dy) = -(m - c), which moves the marker to ndc_P + c. c is a per-eye baseline (`drawtag::LagBaseline`): seeded from the first 30 frames with the head under 3 deg/s, then an EMA (alpha 0.05) of m on frames with the head under 3 deg/s and |m - c| < 0.01 NDC. No patch, and no hold, until it is seeded with a standard deviation under 0.5 px. Reset on a change of b0 row 5 or row 7 scale, the marker target's size, or the eye's matched draw place; 90 slow-head frames in a row over 0.01 NDC start the seed again. Gate |dx|, |dy| < 0.05 NDC. The one-frame hold, the both-eyes rule, the ordinal guard and the one patched B10B draw per eye are unchanged.
- **Rationale.** The flight measured c at about (0.0000, +0.03609) NDC in both eyes, constant across distance and position: a frustum-centre difference between the HUD's and the world's projections, a static calibration. What is left after it is the lag.
- **What changed (body, `fix.native_on_foot_body_align_vertical` = off | on, default off, hidden, live, TEMPORARY).** Body vertical locked to the ground (feet clipped under terrain with pov at F48). With vertical off, body mode drops the body-up component (AMC row 1) of the body run's position and velocity after the filter, so d moves the body only along its right and forward axes; the up trim is left out of the target; F48's standing height is logged, not applied. With vertical on, F48's behaviour.
- **What it logs.** Per eye each second (`marker fix (F50, ...)`): c in NDC and px, its standard deviation and state (seeding, seeded, ready), resets and the last reason; |m - c| in px by head speed (< 3, 3..20, 20..60, >= 60 deg/s); patched draws, held, refused as not ready, gated; the residual after the patch, X0 through the patched rows read back minus (P + c), by head speed (about 0 at every speed if the patch took). The honesty check: m on slow-head frames binned by head pitch (< -15, -15..15, > 15 deg) and roll (|roll| < 10, >= 10 deg) since the last reset; a spread over 2 px between two bins of 30 or more frames logs `the baseline is not constant: masking`, and the fix is not to be claimed. Body: the vertical-off note on each entry to body; once a second the up component d would have had (the target's up in body axes, mean and range) and the per-frame step dropped, beside the standing height.
- **Rig.** native_frame_test: the lag delta lands the marker on ndc_P + c and its gate refuses; the baseline seeds over 30 slow frames, refuses a fast head and a far m, restarts after 90 far frames; the vertical drop.
- **Not done.** Not flown, not installed. The honesty check logs only; it does not stop the patch. The would-be vertical is the target's up (what d converges to with vertical on), not a shadow filter's output. The eye-assignment reset keys on the eye's matched draw place, not on the record index.

### 2026-10-15: F51 (raise the FP rig and the view; the pose freeze's local X and gate)

- **Why (F50 flight, log edvr_gfx_20261015_153136).** With vertical off the feet are on the ground, but the camera sits at upper-chest height and the arms below the shoulders: 3P pov 1.708 m against FP side 1.477 m (body up), so the game's FP rig and camera are about 0.23 m below the 3P body's eye. The pose freeze never ran: "0 for the local X 0000000000000000; no measured call for the local X".
- **What changed (raise, `fix.native_on_foot_body_raise_fp` = on (default) | off; hidden, live, TEMPORARY; body mode only).** dh = (3P povCamera height, or the head plus 0.038 m) - (FP root height), body axes up, measured each frame the inputs and the FP root are fresh, clamped to 0..0.5 m and low-passed (tau 1 s) from 0; held under ADS or a stale input. The applied raise follows dh at 5 cm a frame at most and returns to 0 at the same cap outside body mode or with the key off. View: the eye-align output carries the view run plus a seated (0, -raise, 0) (seated y = -dh raises the camera by dh). FP arms: the body-shift post-hook (0x3DA1DE0) also takes the FP AMC (the mode-2 AMC fpRootTrack latches, the mode re-read in the hook) and moves each record at the AMC origin by raise times the body's up row (AMC row 1, the axis dh is measured on), under the 3P rule (`nofoot::shiftIfAtOrigin`: a moved record is not moved again). The body's vertical stays locked (F50).
- **Pose freeze.** The census takes the kind's X when the Animate hook has counted it in the last 5 to 10 s; else the one X counted whose [X+8] is the local iface's GetPoseData; else (no match, or several X share P, since P may be a skeleton asset every humanoid shares) the kind's X, F49's behaviour. The hook counts every X it sees in a two-bank lock-free table. The gate is now no locomotion: the 3P AMC origin's horizontal speed (low-passed 0.2 s) under 0.35 m/s for 0.3 s, so a weapon out freezes too; ADS stays excluded; F49's head-steady test is logged only. The key and the probe default are unchanged.
- **What it logs.** Once a second in body: `body raise (F51, key ...)`: dh measured, the 3P pov and FP side heights and the pov source, dh low-passed, the applied raise, the seated offset and the eye align published, the FP records shifted and skipped and the passes, the part names shifted, the first shifted record before and after, the up row; then the FP handAttach joints (52 L, 51 R) in world as the skeleton places them and with the arms' shift. Every 5 s with the freeze statistics: the Animate X table's top 8 by calls with P = [X+8] and what it equals (3P or FP GetPoseData, the local X, an iface's X); per latched skeleton, X = *(iface+0x2B8) and *(iface+0x120) with P and Animate counts, [iface+0x2C0], [iface+0xF8], GetPoseData, the X the census takes and why; the gate's speed, ADS and the head test.
- **The weapon.** Not moved by this build. It is not one of the AMC's 54 parts (no weapon name in the part table), so the record shift does not reach it, and what draws it at the handAttach joints is not found. If it follows the skeleton it draws dh below the shifted hands; the hand-attach line gives both places.
- **Rig.** native_frame_test: dh's clamp, low-pass from 0 and hold; seated y = -dh agrees with seatedFromBody and raises the camera; the 5 cm cap; the idempotent shift; the locomotion gate (standing rests, walking does not, a vertical fall is not locomotion, ADS ends a rest).
- **Not done.** Not flown, not installed. The weapon's renderer; the FP records equalling the FP AMC origin (the 3P rule's premise) is unverified for the FP AMC (the shifted and skipped counts say); a P match that is ambiguous takes nothing.

### 2026-10-15: F52 (the marker fix per key; which pass the world rows come from)

- **Why (F50 flight).** The patch read 0.00 px residual at every head speed, and |m - c| was under 0.5 px before it, yet the waypoint moves with the head and an eye dump shows the cyan marker fragments drifting 6 to 17 px against the terrain with the head nearly still. Either the world reference is wrong, or other parts of the marker are not covered: only the tagged key-43 record's B10B draw (one of six B10B composite draws per eye) was patched.
- **What changed (tags).** Every post-stub marker node is tagged: key 10 (the reticle), world markers mode game and posts with no fresh head pose are tagged in place (the game's t at +0x40, low mantissa bits only, guarded reads and writes; their placement is untouched). The tag cells are one per 8-bit tag key (256; F47's 32 put keys 16 apart in one cell). Finds and history raised to 32 and 96.
- **What changed (fix, `fix.native_on_foot_marker_fix`, default off).** Per key: a draw is patched when a key's fresh read-back match names its place in the eye and the same key is matched in the other eye; each key has its own baseline, one-frame hold and find per eye (`drawtag::KeyIndex`, 12 slots). The gate (0.05 NDC), seed (30 slow-head frames), sd under 0.5 px, the hold and the ordinal guard are F50's.
- **What changed (reference, `fix.native_on_foot_marker_ref` = temporal | world_first; hidden, live, TEMPORARY).** temporal (default): the eye's first world pool draw's rows (b1 270..273) with their rotation replaced by the temporal pass's camera rows for the eye (`temporalPassEyeRows`: float 932 of the scene block at the eye's first recognised scene draw), keeping the world rows' projection and eye (`drawtag::swapRotation`); used while the temporal pass gave usable rows within 90 frames, else world_first. A change of the reference in use resets every baseline of the eye (an eye-assignment-style reset). Rows whose axes do not correspond (right or up over 90 degrees, or mirrored) are not used.
- **What it logs.** Per eye each second, `probe (F52): eye N world rows`: the first world pool draw that gave the rows (VS, PS, target size, DSV, viewport, b1, VS changes), world vs temporal (rotation deg mean and max, each axis, the forward sign, px at the newest P, whether the temporal rows' b1 is the world draw's), the reference asked and in use. `probe (F52): keys -> records`: per tag key the record, the B10B draws that read it per eye (places mask), patched per eye; B10B draws per eye tagged, NOT tagged, not copied; posts tagged in place. Per key and eye with activity, `fix (F50/F52, ...)`: F50's line (baseline, |m - c| and the residual by head speed, the honesty check). The F47 line adds the NOT tagged counts per eye.
- **Rig.** native_frame_test: every tag key's sentinel and cell distinct with the round trip; the reference default; the B10B bookkeeping; the key table's reclaim; the bases, the forward sign, the angle, and swapRotation (identity under its own basis, the camera forward at the rows' offset, the eye kept).
- **Not done.** Not flown, not installed. The |m - c| measure runs only with the fix key on (as F50). The temporal rows are the pass's per-eye provenance latch, not the frame's chosen rows (chosen at the first treat, after the marker draws); none at all when temporal AA is off. A draw that reads two records is matched to the first.

### 2026-10-15: F54 (the held weapon: MGMC records measured; shifted with the FP raise)

- **Why.** F51/F53 raise the FP arms (the FP AMC's records) but the held weapon stays put, so it floats off the hands while the raise is on. The static RE (build 332841) names the likely renderer: MotionGraphModelComponent (MGMC). Its submit job 0x43ED820 (thunk 0x43B796A; rcx = the MGMC) skips an object whose mask [obj+0x380] is zero, else writes one 0x150-byte record at obj+0x230 (position obj+0x240..0x248, copy obj+0x354..0x35C) from the instance matrix every run, and hands its pointer to 0x4C837B0 (deferred). The viewmodel byte is obj+0x1C8. Not proven: what the weapon entity is made of is data, not code.
- **What changed.** Two hooks through the F9 relay plumbing (`installF9ProbeInto`), installed in every VR session behind the identity check. The MGMC post-hook (35 prologue bytes verified, 8 stolen: mov rax,rsp; mov [rax+0x20],rsi; push rdi, run by the trampoline after the relay enters the wrapper; the return value forwarded unchanged). The 0x4C837B0 pre-hook (24 bytes verified, 7 stolen; twelve arguments forwarded; the caller from `_ReturnAddress`, since the relay jumps). Both prologues match the offline exe. SHIFT: only while `g_f51FpOn` (raise_fp on, body mode, native stereo on foot, the same gate as the FP AMC shift), an MGMC with mask != 0, byte +0x1C8 != 0, and its record within 0.3 m of FP handAttach 51 or 52 (world, unshifted, published by the frame thread each frame) before the shift moves both position copies by the published FP raise (constant raise_cm, 10 cm, along the up row) after the job returns. Not cumulative: the job rewrites the record each run. Never with mask 0. The hooks do no allocation, logging or locks; reads and writes under SEH; the counters and tables are drained once a second on the frame thread. No new key.
- **What it logs (once a second, native stereo on foot).** `weapon (F54): MGMC submits`: calls, mask 0, unreadable, tabled, the local FP weapon's matched and shifted counts, the raise d, and the four handAttach joints (FP R51 L52, 3P R66 L67, world). Up to 8 `MGMC <ptr>` lines nearest the hands: the byte, the mask, the record position, the distance to each of the four joints, calls and shifts, marked when within 0.3 m with the byte set; a count of the rest. `0x4C837B0 calls` (the AMC's excluded) and one line per caller RVA (known sites named: MGMC, GUI mesh, the five unidentified roots), with calls, records and the record nearest an FP hand (index, pointer, position, distance; up to 256 records scanned a call). Install or refusal lines for each hook. The F51 line's weapon text now points at the F54 lines.
- **Risks.** The muzzle flash and the lights do not follow: they are driven by their own joints or records, not this record. The ammo readout (the AR display, HumanoidARRenderComponent 0x1A14320) is built from the game camera matrix and submitted through the GUI path, camera-space, so it does not follow either. A wrong match (another viewmodel MGMC within 0.3 m) would rise too; the lines show what matched. The joints lag the job by up to a frame (about 3 cm at a walk), inside the 0.3 m match.
- **Rig.** native_frame_test: the 0.3 m match (0.2 and 0.29 m match; 0.35 m and NaN do not), the mask, byte and unknown-joint gates, and the shift moving both record copies by the same d.
- **Not done.** Not flown, not installed. Whether the weapon is an MGMC at all is the flight's question (the per-MGMC lines answer it). Only the 8 MGMCs nearest the hands are listed per second, not every one. The 0x4C837B0 tally records only while native stereo is on foot.

### 2026-10-15: F55 (walking shift, FP shoulder drop experiment, body capture)

- **Why (F53/F54 flight, log edvr_gfx_20261015_173020).** The body chased the locomotion lean: d's horizontal target followed the anchor joint, which moves with locomotion and aim. The flight ran with vertical ON, anchor pov and trims up 6.5 and forward 8.5, not the F53 defaults (Sean is resetting them).
- **What changed (walking shift).** F48's standing capture (`nofoot::StandingHeight`) also captures the 3P rest point's body x and z (the neck on the neck anchor; the anchor's own source otherwise), only at idle: not moving (the F49 gate's rule: under 0.35 m/s for 0.3 s), not ADS, the head steady for 1 s. The key-on capture is the height alone; a change of rest point restarts the window. Once captured, the source's horizontal is the rest plus its offset (`nofoot::walkRestSource`), so d_h = FP side_h - (rest_h + offset_h) is constant in the body frame and the torso leans with the legs. Live x and z until the first idle capture. The F41 filters and the 5 cm cap are unchanged.
- **What changed (experiment (c)).** New hidden live key `fix.native_on_foot_fp_shoulder_drop_cm` (0..20, default 0, TEMPORARY). Above 0, in body mode: the census builds a plan for the FP X (g_cand[1]'s chosen X, with the F51 fallbacks; refused when it is the 3P local X) with the subtree of FP joints 19 and 20; the Animate post-hook (0x741520) moves each member's model matrix position down (FP model -y) by the drop. The weight ramps linearly over 0.2 s, to 0 when ADS starts and back after; the applied drop moves 5 cm a frame at most. The hook runs while the pose freeze key is not off or the drop key is above 0. Default 0 writes nothing.
- **Body vertical.** `fix.native_on_foot_body_align_vertical` defaults to off in code (verified); each body entry logs `body vertical: OFF (feet on the ground)` or `ON`.
- **What it logs.** `walking shift (F55): rest captured (#N ...)` at each capture, and once a second the rest, whether it is used, the live source and d's horizontal target. `body (F55): body axes after d`: per side the FP clavicles and hands (the span snapshot of 19/20 and 37/44 when fresh, else the census by name), the 3P clavicles and hands plus d, the freeze weight, d's up component. `shoulder drop (F55)`: the plan (members, whether 37 and 44 are in it), once a second the world positions of 19, 20, 37, 44 before and after the drop, calls and drops, failures once each.
- **Rig.** native_frame_test (169 checks): d_h constant while the live neck leans; the horizontal capture only at idle (not at key-on, walking or ADS); the drop's clamp (0..20, NaN 0) and its 0.2 s ramp in and out; the subtree of 19 and 20.

The FP model's up is taken as its +y (as F45's yaw-only FP map); the L/R mapping 19/37 and 20/44 is assumed and the plan line logs the joint probe's indices to check it. Temporary keys added: `fix.native_on_foot_fp_shoulder_drop_cm`.

### 2026-10-15: F56 (3P arm IK onto the FP hand attach joints; probe by default)

- **What changed.** New hidden live key `fix.native_on_foot_arm_ik` = off | probe (default) | nudge | on (TEMPORARY). Census (once per local 3P X): each arm's chain from the parents of the hand (F = parent of H, U = parent of F, checked against the clavicle; else an upward search of 4 levels), the groups gU, gF, gH parent first (`nofoot::ikArmChain`), and the 3P skeleton's whole parent array is now dumped like the FP one. The FP Animate post-hook snapshots joints 51, 52, 37 and 44 (whole matrices) before any shoulder-drop write. The game thread's P1 callback publishes G = F_root x Tr(r - d) x inv(M3P) for the local 3P AMC every call (whole FP root matrix; r only while the F51 raise is on). The 3P post-hook's buffer discovery and pre-write copy are now shared: the freeze runs first, its refusals no longer skip the IK, and the IK carries the arm by the freeze's clavicle delta D_C. The solve: T_A = A_fp x G, T_H = inv(L_A) x T_A; the elbow circle of the two-bone solve, swivel from the animated elbow toward 0.5 (animated elbow) + 0.5 (hint: down 0.8, out 0.6, back 0.1), clamped (elbow out at least 10 cm, not above the shoulder); swing-only rotations of U about S and F about E; the hand rotation nlerp'd to the target; forearm and upper-arm helpers rolled by t x the twist. All matrices are computed before the first write; no allocation, logging or locks in the hook; every read and write under SEH; a write fault stands the IK down for the session.
- **Modes and logs.** probe: the solve runs at weight 1 and writes nothing; every 5 s `arm IK (F56, probe)` (calls, base weight, the gate's state, refusals by reason, the FP snapshot and G ages, G publishes), `G inputs` (M3P and F_root row-length error and determinant, r, d, G's rows), and per arm the distance and rotation of T_A against the animated 3P attach, reach (min/mean/max) and shortfall, l1 and l2, the animated elbow angle, the solved elbow and shoulder, clamps, the chain's hand error (about 0 in reach: the math check), the distance gate. `arm IK (F56, probe 5)`: FP R51 in world by the root's yaw only and by the whole matrix, cm apart, the nearest MGMC record to each, and its instance matrix against the FP attach rotation. The census prints the chain, depths, rest l1/l2, groups and the children of U and F with their t. nudge: the left hand group (gH) up 25 cm in model y at the base weight, gU and gF as animated. on: the solve written at the base weight (0.2 s ramp: native on foot, body mode, hide_fp_body and body_unclip on, an arm plan, the FP snapshot and G under 100 ms, no stand-down) times the distance gate (1 under 0.35 m, 0 at 0.8 m); the applied hand offset moves at most 5 cm a call. Outside body mode, and in off and probe, nothing is written. Each held reason and refusal is logged once.
- **Body setting.** Keep `fix.native_on_foot_body_align_vertical` off (feet on the ground). For this mode set Body height +9 (the menu slider); its default is unchanged.
- **Rig.** native_frame_test (181 checks): the two-bone solve (bone lengths kept, the hand on a reachable target, the elbow angle 92.87 deg by the law of cosines), the clamp of an unreachable target (0.999 of the reach, short by the excess) and a folded one, the pole choosing the elbow side, the swivel clamp, the rotation helpers, the 0.2 s ramp, the distance gate, the 5 cm cap, and the chain derivation with its refusals.
- **Risks.** (a) The skin may not read the Animate span: the nudge decides it, and if nothing moves the IK is dead until the row packer is found. (b) The FP and 3P attach frames may differ by a constant rotation (probe 2's rotation would be the same every frame; fix with a per-side constant). (c) The whole FP root against its yaw only is unresolved (probe 5). (d) Elbow through the torso: the swivel clamp, unflown. (e) Fingers keep the 3P animation's curl. (f) With the pose freeze on, an arm whose gate is 0 is left at the freeze's rest pose while the other is solved; the freeze defaults to probe. (g) The FP Animate may run a frame after the 3P one: 1 to 2 cm of lag at 100 deg/s.
- **Not done.** Not flown, not installed. Whether the skin reads the hook's writes. The swivel is blended from the animated elbow by the weight (a small addition to the spec: the elbow does not jump when the weight leaves 0). No extrapolation of the FP target, no copy of the FP finger pose, no per-side frame constant, no weapon gate. Temporary keys added: `fix.native_on_foot_arm_ik`.

### 2026-10-15: F57 part 1 (arm IK refinements: FP elbow pole, torso square-up, elbow limits, three sliders)

- **Why (F56 flight, log 193220, as reported).** The skin reads the hook's writes and the right hand lands on the grip (plan U 40/41, F 45/46, H 58/59). The 3P rifle stance yaws the shoulder line about 40 deg (the left shoulder 14 cm inboard), and the pole followed the animated, bladed elbow, so the left elbow crossed the chest; the swivel clamp fired 449 of 449.
- **What changed.** (1) The FP Animate snapshot adds joints 19, 20, 25, 26, 31, 34 (`nofoot::kIkFpJoints`). Pole = unit(perp(unit(E' - S'), axis T' - S')) with S', E', T' the FP upper arm, forearm and hand mapped by G; under 0.2 (a nearly straight FP arm) the F56 pole stands. The 0.2 is taken on the unit upper-arm direction (dimensionless, as F56's), not on metres. The elbow-out clamp stays. (2) Square-up: theta = yaw(3P 36 - 35) - yaw(FP 20 - 19 by G) in xz, the correction clamped to +-50 deg, ramped over 0.3 s (frame thread, on only) and scaled by the amount slider, about the vertical through joint 34, split 1/3, 2/3, 1 over the exclusive subtrees of spine 16, 21, 24 (rigid per joint). It runs after the freeze and before the arm solve; the arm groups' M0 is the squared pose. The census adds the chest root (parent of 35 and 36, required to be 24) and the spine 16 subtree by level to `pfz::ArmPlan` (`nofoot::ikSquarePlan`); any refusal turns off the square-up alone. When it writes, every spine joint not written by the solve takes its squared pose (an unsolved arm rides its clavicle). (3) The elbow's interior angle is held in 40..170 deg through the two-bone D range; the range widens toward the animated arm's D by (1 - weight), so nothing jumps at weight 0. (4) Sliders (Native on foot page, live, TEMPORARY): Elbow swivel `fix.native_on_foot_arm_elbow_swivel_trim_deg` (-90..90, 0; positive swings the elbow out, added before the clamp), Torso square-up `fix.native_on_foot_arm_square_amount` (0..1, 1), Elbow out `fix.native_on_foot_arm_elbow_out_cm` (0..30, 10; replaces the constant). Writes only with `arm_ik = on` in body mode; probe still writes nothing; nudge never squares.
- **What it logs (every 5 s).** `arm IK (F57): arm L/R`: swivel source (FP elbow vs fallback counts), the clamp's fire rate at the slider values, the solved elbow's interior angle min/mean/max and how often a limit held the hand off its target, the shoulder S |x| after the square-up, the last FP elbow in 3P beside the solved elbow. `square-up`: theta min/mean/max, clamps, the rotation applied (probe: what the full ramp would apply), amount, ramp, calls that wrote it. `mapping vs the weapon`: R51 and L52 through the whole FP root and its yaw only, the nearest MGMC record to each, and which is closer (R51's is the grip). The census prints the square-up plan once per X.
- **Rig.** native_frame_test (188 checks): the FP pole's fallback, the yaw and its +-50 deg clamp, the 0.3 s ramp, the spine plan's levels and the 1/3, 2/3, 1 split about the pivot, the 40..170 deg limits, the elbow-out slider in the clamp and the sliders' clamps.
- **Not done.** Not flown, not installed. The hand-roll constant K is dropped (not implemented). Whether the square-up's 40 deg turn reads right on the skin (the shoulders' deformation) and whether the FP pole's elbow sits outside the chest are the flight's questions.

### 2026-10-15: F57 part 2 (cb1 version census, measure only, rides the marker probe)

- **What changed.** The marker probe's tees (`fix.native_on_foot_marker_probe = on`; no new key) follow every draw's VS b1 (the binding shadow): up to 8 b1 buffers with their Map and Unmap versions and, at each Unmap, rows 270..273 copied from the mapping under SEH before the real Unmap. Per frame, up to 32 distinct contents, each with the frame's b1 Unmap that wrote it (0: carried from an earlier frame) and, per eye (the draw's depth view; else unknown), the first draw index that used it and that draw's VS, PS and family: terrain or planet patch, sky, atmosphere, pool or scene, HUD, temporal (the temporal pass's source VS), other. The eye's main content is the one its first main-scene draw used (`drawtag::sceneRefWhy`). Nothing drawn, bound or written changes.
- **What it logs (once a second).** `cb1 versions (F57, measure only)`: frames, distinct contents a frame, b1 Maps, Unmaps and UpdateSubresource a frame, draws a frame and the table overflows, frames with no main content per eye. Per eye, `cb1 versions (F57): eye N roles`: per (family, VS, PS) the frames, the first draw index range, the Unmap order, whether it is the main content, and its forward and rotation angles against the main content by head speed (< 3, 3..20, 20..60, >= 60 deg/s). A second camera sample shows as a non-main role whose angle grows with head speed.
- **Not done.** Not flown. The family hashes for sky and atmosphere come from older docs (sky VS F8FA801F2CB1E27C and 1F8D73118F42ED8B; the four atmosphere hashes matched as VS or PS) and are unverified on foot. A b1 is learned at its first draw, so its first frame reads as unknown rows. A frame is the Present boundary's (any tee turns it). Buffers past 8 are counted, not followed.

### 2026-10-15: F58 (full-3P head follow, spine bend toward the head)

- **What changed (head follow).** In body mode with `fix.native_on_foot_hide_fp_body` and `fix.native_on_foot_body_unclip` both on (full 3P), the body target's horizontal (right, forward) on the neck and pov anchors comes from the job camera (it carries the HMD's translation), not the FP root: the same body axes, the F41 filters, the trims and the 5 cm cap. The up stays the FP root's, and the F50 vertical lock still drops it (feet on the ground). The clavicle anchor is unchanged. Outside full 3P nothing changes.
- **What changed (spine bend).** New hidden live key `fix.native_on_foot_spine_bend` = on (default) | off (TEMPORARY), and the slider Spine bend amount `fix.native_on_foot_spine_bend_amount` (0..1, default 1, Native on foot page). It acts only in full-3P body mode. In the Animate post-hook, after the freeze and before the square-up and the arm IK: the pov joint (the census's def_c_povCamera_joint index) is turned toward the eye target T (the job camera less the body run's applied d, in body axes = the 3P model space, published by the frame thread each tick) about the spine root (joint 16's position). The pitch about the body's right axis matches the sagittal angle of pov - pivot to T - pivot; the roll about the forward axis then matches the frontal angle (`nofoot::bendSolve`). A rotation keeps the pivot distance, so a pure height mismatch gives no bend. Clamped forward 0..45, back 0..15, roll +-20 deg; times the 0.2 s ramp (key on, full 3P, native on foot, a fresh AMC sample with the pov joint, not ADS) and the amount; the applied angles move at most 5 cm of pov motion a call. Split 1/3, 2/3, 1 over the spine levels 16, 21, 24 (the square-up's plan, chain verified from the parents) about the one pivot, on M0, so the square-up and the arm solve run on the bent pose. The bent spine list is written with the square-up's write (an arm the IK does not write rides it). The bend rides the arm IK's hook path: `pfz::g_ikWanted` is now also set by the bend key in full 3P, and an FP-snapshot or G refusal skips only the arms and the square-up. No allocation, logging or locks in the hook; reads and writes under SEH (the existing guarded write; a fault stands the IK and the bend down).
- **What it logs (once a second, body mode or while the bend runs).** `spine bend (F58)`: the head follow's source (the FP root, the job camera (full 3P), the job camera (FP root not fresh), the FP clavicles); the bend's state, amount, ramp; hook calls, bent and written; the residual eye target - pov (cm) before and after the bend, mean, max and (forward, up, right); pitch and roll applied (mean, min, max) and solved before the clamps; clamp hits forward 45, back 15, roll 20; the pov joint and its spine level; refusals (plan, target torn, target stale, pov, degenerate, singular). The key's change line.
- **Rig.** native_frame_test (193 checks): the residual split, the solve (pitch +20 and roll +10 land the pov on the target; a height mismatch and a degenerate pov give none), the clamps (45, 15, 20; NaN 0), the 1/3, 2/3, 1 split for pitch and roll, the 0.2 s ramp, the 5 cm cap.
- **Not done.** Not flown, not installed. The residual's "after" is the bend at the applied angles; the height and anything past the clamps is left (logged, not moved). Risk: the census's neck and pov feed the body target and the F55 rest capture, so if the census reads the bent span, d and the bend share the same residual (watch for a slow drift in the walking shift lines). The bend needs the arm plan (one arm at least) and the square-up plan; either refused leaves the bend off (refusal `plan`). Temporary keys added: `fix.native_on_foot_spine_bend`, `fix.native_on_foot_spine_bend_amount`.

### 2026-10-16: F59 (the bend on the neck, square-up to the FP forward, clavicle reach assist, ADS, the waypoint dump)

- **Why (F57/F58 flight, log edvr_gfx_20261016_021950).** The spine bend never ran: 246 of 246 lines refused with `pov, spine level 255`, because the pov joint (15) is a child of the root 7, not under the spine root 16. The square-up's reference, the FP clavicles, sit only 7.5 cm apart. The left elbow-out clamp fired 54 to 100% because it measured from the midline.
- **What changed.** (1) The bend turns the neck joint 34 (under 24) toward T = (job camera - d) in body axes + the rest offset (0, -0.155, -0.161), gated on the neck census's head sample (not the pov joint), and stays on in ADS. (2) Square-up: theta = yaw(the 3P upper-arm line U40 -> U41) - 90 deg relative to the FP forward (G's z row on xz); the +-50 deg clamp, the 0.3 s ramp and the spine split are unchanged. (3) Clavicle reach assist (every mode but nudge), before swing 1: past 0.97 (l1 + l2) the shoulder is asked to move toward the target by min(excess + 3 cm, 10 cm), times the per-arm weight, 5 cm a call while writing; gU, gF and gH turn rigidly about the clavicle, at most 25 deg. M0 is restored for an arm that is not written, so the spine write never carries the assist. (4) Elbow out: out x (E.x - S.x) >= 6 cm from the arm's own shoulder; the slider's default is now 6 (it was 10). (5) ADS (journalAds, 0.15 s, frame thread `g_ikAdsW`): the square-up amount, elbow out and swivel trim lerp to their ADS sliders; the distance gate goes from 0.35..0.8 m to 0.6..1.2 m; a shoulder raise and forward push ride the clavicle assist; a forward lean is added to the bend's pitch before its clamps. Six live sliders on the Native on foot page (TEMPORARY): `fix.native_on_foot_arm_ads_square_amount` (0..1, 1), `_arm_ads_elbow_out_cm` (0..20, 6), `_arm_ads_swivel_trim_deg` (-60..60, 0), `_arm_ads_shoulder_raise_cm` (0..10, 3), `_arm_ads_shoulder_forward_cm` (0..10, 5), `_arm_ads_lean_deg` (0..15, 6). (6) Waypoint dump, measure only: with `fix.native_on_foot_marker_probe = on`, the eye-dump key also takes 16 consecutive frames of both eyes, a 512x512 crop per frame and eye about the probe's ndc_marker (the drawn marker, read back 2..3 frames late, under 500 ms old; else P through the scene rows), of the final eye and of the same frame before the UI layer's composite: `eyes\eye_<stamp>_mk_eye<e>_f<kk>.bmp`, `..._mk_pre_eye<e>_f<kk>.bmp` and `eye_<stamp>_mk.csv` (the centre's source, both ndc values and their ages, the box).
- **What it logs.** `spine bend (F58)` (now the neck: bent and written counts, refusals by reason, the residual before and after) and `spine bend (F59)` (the ADS lean and weight). Every 5 s: `arm IK (F59): square-up` (theta against the FP forward), `after the square-up` (the yaw left, upper arm |x| L and R, their spacing, the F57 clavicle theta beside it, the ADS weight), per arm `arm IK (F59): arm L/R` (the own-shoulder clamp rate, the assist's count, excess, move and turn, its 25 deg caps; in ADS the reach after the assist, shortfall, per-arm weight, gate, interior angle, clamps). The full vs yaw-only mapping against the weapon is the F57 line, unchanged. The ADS sliders' change line. `eye capture (F59): waypoint dump` when armed and when written.
- **Rig.** native_frame_test (198 checks): the square-up to the forward (30 deg right gives +30 and lands the line on the forward's right; a bladed 40; a vertical forward refuses), the assist's move (4.65 cm, the 10 cm cap, 0 in reach) and its 25 deg cap (10 cm on a 15 cm clavicle), the own-shoulder clamp on both sides, the 0.15 s ADS ramp, the ADS gate and the sliders' lerp and clamps.
- **Not done.** Not flown, not installed. The waypoint dump needs the marker probe on and the eye-dump key; the pre-UI crop comes from the native sharpen door (before the layer's composite), so with no UI layer it is the same image as the final one. The ndc_marker centre lags 2..3 frames (the read-back); the CSV carries both centres so a miss can be measured. Whether the shoulders deform well under the assist's 25 deg turn (only gU, gF, gH turn; the clavicle joint itself does not) is the flight's question. Temporary keys added: the six `_arm_ads_*` sliders.

### 2026-10-16: F59b (the full-3P head follow through ADS, its fallback, the eye - neck residual)

- **Cause of the ADS dropout (F58 log edvr_gfx_20261016_021950, scratchpad f58.txt; code).** The body frame was there: 40 of the 49 `head follow ... from none` seconds were ADS with a fresh AMC sample and the pov joint (the F58 state `ramping out: ADS` is reached only past the `no fresh AMC sample` and `no pov joint` tests). `alignTick`'s wait chain ends `else if (ads) wait = "held: aiming down sights"`, so in ADS the body-target block never ran (`g_f58FollowSrc` stayed -1, printed `none (no body frame ...)`) and `bodyFilter` got err = 0: d did not hold, it decayed to 0 at tau 0.1 s (5 cm a frame), so the 3P body went back to its own place for the whole aim. The other 9 seconds were a stale AMC sample (over 100 ms; `held: no fresh AMC sample`).
- **What changed.** Full 3P only (body mode, `hide_fp_body` and `body_unclip` on; `f58Full3P` is now computed before the waits): (1) ADS no longer waits; the target runs through it as outside it (the F48 standing window gets `ads`, so ADS is still never a rest). (2) The body frame (`nofoot::followPick`): live (a fresh sample with the head census and the pov; it becomes the last good frame); else the AMC matrix (a fresh sample without the census: its matrix, job camera and root, the last good frame's 3P joints carried into it through their body coordinates, `nofoot::carryBody`); else the last good frame itself; never one older than 0.5 s (`kFollowHoldMs`); past it, none (the old waits). (3) A change between two sources ramps the target from the last output over 0.25 s (`nofoot::FollowBlend`; the change frame outputs the last output exactly); to or from none is immediate, as every wait was. The bend still gets the sample as read. No hook changed; no game memory is read by the new code. Outside full 3P nothing changes.
- **What it logs (once a second, full 3P, on the F58 line's tick).** `head follow (F59b): head follow source: job camera | amc matrix | last good frame (N ms) | none`, the frames per source this second, the oldest frame used, the ramp, ADS. `eye - neck residual after the shift, the bend and the square-up` (body axes right, up, forward; eye = the job camera): from the hook's published post-bend neck 34 (its (T - N1) means; T = (eye - d) + rest, so eye - (N1 + d) = (T - N1) - rest; the square-up turns about 34's vertical and leaves 34 where the bend put it), cm per axis and the norm of the means, bend calls and written; and the same less the rest offset (0, 15.5, 16.1). When no bend call measured it: `the hook's post-correction neck is not available this second ... not inferred`. A second line: eye - (census neck + d), per-frame mean and norm mean; whether the census's GetModelMatrix reads the hook's bent pose is not verified (equal to the hook's figure means it does).
- **Rig.** native_frame_test (201 checks): the pick (live wins; matrix or last up to 500 ms, 501 is none; no last frame is none), the ramp (no jump on the change frame, half at 0.125 s, there at 0.25 s; to and from none immediate), the body-axes residual on a 90 deg yaw, `carryBody`, and (T - N1) - rest = eye - (N1 + d).
- **Not done.** Not flown, not installed. The hook's residual exists only while the bend runs (the key on, full 3P, a plan); with the bend off only the census figure is logged. The residual is per-second means of the hook's (T - N1), not a per-frame norm. ADS in full 3P now moves the body with the head where F58 held it at 0: whether that suits aiming is the flight's question. No key added.

### 2026-10-16: F60 (the body fitted to the weapon; the view never moved)

- **What changed.** The 3P Animate post-hook (every mode but nudge, both arms planned with their T_A) publishes a weapon-fit measurement (seqlock, one writer): g = mean(T_A left, T_A right) (the FP handAttach 52 and 51 through G), f = G's z row on xz (unit), Sd = g - f (0.82 (l1 + l2)) - up 0.10 m (`nofoot::kFitReach`, `kFitDropM`; l1 + l2 the mean of both arms, 0.59 m outside 0.2..1.5 m), plus the ADS shoulder raise (up) and forward push (along f) times the ADS weight; Umid = the mean of U 40 and 41 after the bend and the square-up, before the clavicle assist; d_w = G's d (body axes) + (Sd - Umid). Since G subtracts d, d_w does not depend on the d it was measured at (open loop). The frame thread (`f60FitStep`): a weapon is held when the publication is under 100 ms old and both distance gates are open; the ramp (0.3 s in and out) rises in full-3P body mode with `arm_ik = on`, its base weight above 0 and the slider above 0, and is zeroed at once outside that; fit = slider x ramp. In `alignTick` the body target (body axes) becomes lerp(d_head, d_w, fit) after the F41 low-passes and trims (d_w carries no trim); horizontal only with the vertical off (the head's up kept; the F50 lock drops it). Then the F59b source blend, bodyFilter (tau 0.1 s, 5 cm a frame, 0.6 m clamp), no integral. New live slider Body fit to weapon `fix.native_on_foot_body_fit_weapon` (0..1, step 0.05, 0.7; Native on foot page, TEMPORARY). The hook allocates, logs and locks nothing; it reads only its own copies.
- **What it logs (once a second, body mode, full 3P, `arm_ik` not off).** `weapon fit (F60)`: the fit weight (mean, and now as slider x ramp, with the held reason), frames with a weapon held, d_head, d_w, the fitted target and the applied d (body axes right, up, forward, cm), the shoulder-mid error |Sd - Umid| after the fit (mean, max, its horizontal and its up part: the vertical remainder, left to the bend), the last Sd, Umid and G's d, the arm length, the smaller gate, ADS and the hook's publications. The F59b eye - neck residual and the F59 per-arm reach lines are unchanged. The slider's change line.
- **Design.** The view is never moved: no eyeAlign, no seated offset, the view run untouched. The body fits the weapon: the 3P shoulders go where the FP grips need them, and the arm IK reaches the rest. Torso facing: the F59 square-up already turns the upper-arm line square to the FP forward (the weapon's forward) in every IK mode, scaled by its own F57 amount slider; it does not follow fit and no key was added for it.
- **Rig.** native_frame_test (206 checks): Sd from a known grip pair and forward (pitched, yawed, ADS; a vertical forward refuses), d_w and its independence of d, the blend at fit 0, 1 and 0.5 (the head's up kept with the vertical off; fit clamped), the 0.3 s ramp in and out, k_reach 0.82, the drop, the arm fallback and the slider's clamp.
- **Not done.** Not flown, not installed. Coupling: the bend's target uses d and Umid includes the bend, so d_w moves a little with d through the bend (no integral; the filter damps it); watch the fit line for a drift. The weapon test is the grips' freshness and the distance gates, not a weapon-held flag; the unarmed FP hands also have grips, so whether fit runs unarmed is the flight's question. Temporary keys added: `fix.native_on_foot_body_fit_weapon`.

### 2026-10-16: F61 (rotation calibration: does a seated yaw or pitch reach the game camera's orientation?)

- **What changed.** New temporary key `fix.native_on_foot_rot_calibrate` = off | on (default off, hidden, live). On, while native stereo is on foot, the d3d11 frame thread (`rotCalTick`) publishes a scheduled seated rotation: yaw +5 deg about the head for 1 s, rest 1 s, pitch +5 deg about the head's right axis for 1 s, rest 1 s, repeating (`nofoot::rotCalStep`, F40's calibrate shape; no loop, no guard). It rides version 9 frame output in the retired slots `reservedOffset[0..1]` (radians; `reservedOffsetEnabled` stays 0, so no version bump and no older runtime reads it). Off publishes zero, and the runtime turns the origin back.
- **Runtime (`rotPoll`, after `eyePoll` in `seatPoll`, no frame open).** The yaw turns `lookSeated` about the vertical through the head, as `lookPoll` does, with the same `eyeApplied` fold. **The pitch path was not wired** (a `SeatPose` carries yaw only), so it was added: an overlay that turns the seated space about the head's right axis through the head (`seatPosePitched`, composed at every `seated.replace` by `seatedPoseFor`; zero pitch is exactly `xrFromSeatPose`, so the default is unchanged). The pivot and axis are the head's when the rotation leaves zero and are kept until it returns, so the return is exact. `lookRebase` zeroes the applied rotation. Each apply is traced: `rot_calibrate_runtime` with the applied yaw and pitch and the head's seated yaw and pitch before and after.
- **What it logs (d3d11, per 1 s step).** `rot calibrate (F61): step ...`: the commanded rotation before and after; the change in orientation (dyaw, dpitch, in each source's own axes: yaw + = turned left, pitch + = up), the mean over the step's last 0.5 s against the 0.5 s before it began, from (1) the list-B camera C (`g_worldC`, rows 0..2 read as right, up, forward), (2) the scene rows cb1 270..273 (the engine-motion source camera, `engineVelocityLastSourceCameraRows`: forward is the clip w column, right and up the clip x and y columns) and (3) the runtime's render head; the head's own motion (render + the command's change: a seated rotation of +a turns the reported head by -a); per-step frame and missing-input counts. After each yaw and pitch step: `rotation reaches the game camera: yes|no; gain X; sign Y`, the camera from cb1 when measured, else C; residual = camera change - head's own motion; no under 1 deg; gain = |residual / command|; sign - means the camera turned with the reported head.
- **Verdict.** Not yet measured.
- **Rigs.** native_frame_test (209 checks): the schedule, the three sources' deltas (left turn yaw +10, rise pitch +10), the head subtraction and the verdict rule (0.9 deg no, 1 deg yes, gain and sign). openxr_native_test (`rot_calibrate_cases.h`): the pitch overlay leaves the head in place and turns its seated pitch by -5 deg with its yaw unchanged; zero is the pose; +5 then -5 is the pose again.
- **Not done.** Not flown, not installed. The C axes' labels (row 0 right, row 1 up) are assumed; cb1 is the definitive one. The engine-motion source camera may be either eye; the step lines carry its sample count. The pitch is not folded into `eyeApplied`: fly with body align off. Temporary keys added: `fix.native_on_foot_rot_calibrate`.

### 2026-10-16: F62a (the fit gated by raised-ness, lateral authority, trims on the final d, bend limits, ADS reach; the marker dump series)

- **Why (F60 flight, log edvr_gfx_20261016_034818, scratchpad arm_ik\f60.txt).** Unarmed, the grip gate sat at 0.0..0.2 with the hands hanging and the fit still ran at 0.7 (F60 needed only gateMin > 0), pulling the body 18..25 cm back; the FP rifle sits about 30 cm right of the eye, so the midpoint rule moved the body right and the bend rolled it about -20 deg; the trims reached the body only 35%; a -173 deg roll was solved in a degenerate case; in ADS the right arm folded to the 40 deg limit.
- **What changed (full-3P body mode with `arm_ik = on`; nothing outside it).** (1) `f60FitStep`: fit = slider x ramp x smoothstep(0.5, 1.0, gateMin) (`nofoot::fitRaised`); the ramp no longer tests the gate. In `alignTick` the fit disengages as the horizontal |d_w - d_head| goes 0.15 to 0.35 m (`fitDisengage`, 1 - smoothstep). (2) Lateral weight: new live slider Body fit sideways `fix.native_on_foot_body_fit_lateral` (0..1, 0.25, Native on foot page, TEMPORARY); the forward keeps Body fit to weapon; the horizontal pull |w (d_w - d_head)| is clamped to 0.12 m along its direction (`nofoot::fitApply`). (3) Trims on the final d: d_head is kept untrimmed, the pull added, the trims subtracted once (no fit: exactly the old target). (4) Bend (Animate hook): while the applied fit weight is above 0 (frame thread `g_fitBendW`, one tick late) the limits are forward and back 8, roll 6 deg (`bendLimits`); with |T - N| before the bend above 0.35 m it is suspended: want 0, the applied angles return at their 5 cm cap (`bendSuspend`; whenever the bend runs). (5) Eye-to-neck in full 3P: `kNeckRestOffsetFull3P` (0, -0.155, -0.09) for the head follow's neck source, the bend's target and the eye - neck residual; outside full 3P the neck anchor keeps (0, -0.155, -0.161). (6) ADS: `_arm_ads_shoulder_forward_cm` defaults to 0 (was 5; code and edvr.ini); `fitShoulderTarget` drops the forward push and takes k_reach = `fitReachK(adsW)` (0.82 to 1.0); d_w's lateral is blended over 0.3 s at each change of the ADS flag (`FitAdsLat`). The 5 cm cap, the filters and the 0.3 s ramp are unchanged; the hook allocates, logs and locks nothing.
- **What it logs (once a second, with the F60 line).** `weapon fit (F62a): weights`: forward and lateral weight means, the sliders, ramp and raised factor now, the raised factor and grip gate means, frames applied, the disengage factor, d_w - d_head and the pull (right, forward, cm), the pull's max and clamped frames, the shoulder-mid residual Sd - Umid (right, forward), the ADS lateral blend's frames. `weapon fit (F62a): bend`: the bend's calls and bent counts, calls at the fit's limits and their hits (8, 8, 6), all hits, suspended calls and the before-residual max; per arm in ADS (weight over 0.5) the reach after the assist (mean, max) and the elbow's interior angle (mean, min); k_reach. The F58 line adds the fit-limited and suspended counts; the F60 line prints the raised factor and the k_reach in use.
- **Marker dump: the cause.** The F59 series did fire: log 034818 has `waypoint dump armed` and `(complete)` for all three runs (035139, 035256, 035828), and the Epic eyes folder holds 62 of 64 crops each, named `eye_<stamp>_mk_eye<e>_f<kk>.bmp` and `_mk_pre_`, beside the CSV; a search for "marker" found only F53's single `eye_<stamp>_L0_marker.bmp`. Each run lost frame 15's right eye (final and pre): the CSV gives its projection 1828 ms (and 1672 ms) old, because the age was taken on the wall clock at the capture and the eye run's own synchronous write (`writeEyeRun`, 03:51:40.26 to 42.06) sits in that frame's right-eye pass; the 3 s deadline was 0.2 s from cutting the run.
- **Marker dump: the fix (temporal_pass.cpp, read only).** Files renamed `eye_<stamp>_marker_eye<e>_f<kk>.bmp`, `eye_<stamp>_marker_pre_eye<e>_f<kk>.bmp`, `eye_<stamp>_marker.csv` (a `late_ms` column added); the projection's age is taken at the frame's first capture, so a stall inside the frame does not age it; the deadline is 10 s. Lines `eye capture (F62a): marker dump series armed` (the folder and file names) and `... series <stamp> (<why>)` (frames, sequences and whether consecutive, final and pre crops of 32, the files); with fewer than 32 final crops a self-check line gives the count per reason (not reached, not tried, no fresh projection, staging, write) and the first six missing crops with their ages.
